Outer flex may align the whole cost, but the individual glyph must retain that native line-height/baseline behavior. Update the focused regression to distinguish painted pseudo-glyph placement from wrapper equality. Recheck the five exact costs at 466px, a monocolor run, no-font fallback, Landfall no-wrapper isolation, syntax and whitespace. No M source audit, full 183-link run, L83 run, broad Friends-forever harness or full gate chain is justified.

## Independent RobQA failure — candidate `68ae0c8f47c08838bcc511972d3bef74ce8be5ae`

- Execution: separate independent reviewer; QA-4 semantic/evidence qualification with bounded QA-2 integration. Exact baseline `48f071a9b16a5b748f2515b1e51236feeeb95ece`. Verdict: **RobQAPass FAIL**; no PASS or Owner acceptance is inferred for this candidate.
- Finding 1, acceptance-violating evidence integrity: Coven Q3 and the packet claimed a successful 59-printing HTTP 200 capture with SHA-256 `cc100d...`, but the committed `released-paper-printings-page-1.json` was the 333-byte Scryfall 429 response with SHA-256 `e3ce34...`; its page receipt correctly recorded 429. This contradicted the claimed complete authoritative recovery and hash/receipt completeness.
- Finding 2, evidence-scope qualification: Celebration treated Overcooked as included released-paper red access even though its captured Mystery Booster Commander Edition printing carries an acorn security stamp. Ordinary-paper protocol explicitly excludes acorn cards.
- Finding 3, grant-versus-payoff qualification: Convoke treated Joyful Stormsculptor as a blue-red explicit grant and used it in the UR combination. Its captured Oracle text only rewards casting a spell that already has convoke; it neither has nor grants convoke.
- Independent bounded crosschecks found no additional defect: all other positive explicit grants/token sources were genuine; other included ordinary-paper records had no acorn/silver/plane/token/scheme/Heroes/playtest leakage; single-face attributed colors matched captured card colors; Convert/Craft and Conjure face handling, Commander ninjutsu/Covercast/Companion combination-only treatment, and the named exclusions/corrections agreed with captured facts. All 43 A/B projected records deep-equal the baseline. Browser execution was deferred because a semantic candidate failure already required a corrected commit.
- Required correction: preserve the original 429 evidence honestly and bind Coven to a separate complete recovered capture; exclude Overcooked from ordinary access; exclude Joyful Stormsculptor as a payoff and remove any now-unsupported UR Convoke combination. Regenerate and submit one new exact candidate for independent re-review.

Expected: archival notes cannot be mistaken for original evidence. Relabel each retained file as a superseded research note/non-authority and point to its actual shared capture; do not delete it or promote it into `sources`.

Generated ignored local `_site/` preview and temporary research scripts are permitted. Existing three research JSON packets and all original captures remain unchanged.

## Acceptance
1. Exactly 25 protocol-versioned records, every WUBRG decision explicit with status/route/confidence, concrete evidence and closest-alternative reasoning. Existing evidence may be referenced with an explicit field mapping; original queries/receipts/cutoffs remain distinguishable from current judgments. Preserve user-directed Annihilator.
2. Split released paper, digital, preview and special/playtest populations; distinguish grants, relevant faces, multicolor joint evidence and reprints. No count-to-tier shortcuts. Unresolved observed access stays null; incomplete searches cannot support absence.
3. Use E1/E2 only with original scope support and capture receipts; disclose missing captures rather than fabricate them. Absorb limited-sample route Low; Affinitycycling singleton alone no tier. General Abandon/Activate/Attach actions not ranked; Attach related-effect citation remains separate.
4. Deterministic producer projects the new records, including per-color statuses/confidence/route and current judgment date, while retaining evidence links. Preview reuses WUBRG symbols, labels source-informed versus card-derived/user-selected appropriately, and displays unresolved Colorless without Primary. No 2021 data/identity/calculation mutation.
5. Independent QA-4 reviews all 25 judgments and consistency, required cheap gate chain and targeted browser coverage. Rebuild and refresh local preview at committed candidate. Batch review table lists old/new placements, rationale and open limitations.

- Parse/shape/reference and scope checks of research records; validate full 25-record coverage and field mapping; producer --check and canonical source bindings.
- Existing source/taxonomy/fixture/recon gate chain; protected-path Git comparison with baseline; meaningful browser assertions for protocol labels, symbols, null/Colorless states, rationale, 2021 isolation and loading/invalid input.
- No broad unrelated wheel/geometry suite. Owner reviews the revised content in local preview.

### Independent RobQA — protocol v1.0 application, 2026-10-27

- Mode/class/verdict: separate non-implementing reviewer; **QA-4, `qa-failed`** for exact candidate `bd1c8ddbe9134b47483416c774dc4e35b496c6f2` over baseline `130e095`. Four acceptance-violating findings are below. QA wrote only `HANDOFF.md`; it made no protocol record, capture, generator, payload, UI, harness, branch, commit, publication, push, merge or deployment change. Draft record status is honest during QA and is not itself a finding.
- Candidate/scope evidence: `git rev-parse HEAD` resolved to the named candidate. Git reports 54 changed paths, all inside the declared envelope. The complete diff is 69,413 insertions/346 deletions. Raw archived HTML/text captures retain source whitespace by design; `git diff --check` over all non-capture authored/material paths is clean. Frozen 2021 runtime/source/fixtures/recon, all three original research packets and existing supplement/example owners are diff-zero.
- **Major — preview authorization is contradicted and internal workflow leaks into player copy.** All nine core revisions say `Not authorized by this record; coordinator controls import`; all ten extended revisions say `No import/integration authorization`, although the user explicitly authorized applying v1.0 to all 25 and updating the preview. Eight core records (Adamant, Adapt, Addendum, Affinity, Afflict, Afterlife, Aftermath and Airbend) also project `Draft record awaits independent RobQA; no application import authorization` through `limitations` into the player-facing payload. Preserve `draft` until QA passes, but remove workflow-status prose from player limitations and record the actual user-authorized preview import separately from any still-unauthorized publication/integration.
- **Major — card-role vocabulary is not protocol-conformant.** Protocol v1.0 fixes `native`, `explicit_grant`, `joint_only`, `recipient_only`, `incidental_mention` and `false_positive`. Twenty retained cards instead use legacy `support` (12), `unrelated` (7) or `grant` (Yume, Chronicler of Valor). Concrete affected records are Alliance, Ascend, Assemble, Assist, Augment and Awaken. Normalize those labels without changing inclusion/eligibility: the grant must be `explicit_grant`, support/recipient-only evidence must use the applicable fixed role, and unrelated/name-only candidates must use the applicable excluded role. The producer currently validates decision references but does not reject this vocabulary drift.
- **Major — two Primary/Secondary boundaries lack the protocol's required qualitative distinction.** In Assist, black Primary and green Secondary are both supported by one Battlebond release, common/uncommon coverage, and the same creature-plus-sorcery role spread; black's rationale differs only by one additional creature. That is a count boundary, not a demonstrated restriction or role distinction. In Awaken, black has three distinct original spell roles across common/uncommon/rare, yet is called Secondary only because its spread is smaller than white/blue; no constraint separates that evidence from the record's own Primary rubric. Reassess these tiers under E3 and state a qualitative restriction if one exists; QA does not prescribe the replacement tier. Amass's blue/red role restrictions, Amplify's white rarity/tribal boundary, Assemble's red-specific Rigger identity and Assist red's same-role creature limitation were independently reviewed and are defensible without a count-only rule.
- **Major — retained query receipt paths have no single resolvable base.** All 28 core `queries[].receipt_paths` resolve only relative to `docs/mechanics-research/` (for example `../../../a-derived/...`); all 31 extended paths resolve only relative to repository root (for example `../a-derived/...`). No field declares a per-packet base, so the 59 receipts cannot all resolve under one documented rule. Normalize to one stated convention or add an explicit base. This is distinct from original-source captures: all 88 declared `sources[].capture.files` references independently resolved under their intended paths and matched their recorded SHA-256 values in the checkout; coordinator Git-blob verification of the 42 new HTML/text/PDF files is separate evidence.
- Passing structural/source checks retained for rework: the three canonical files contain exactly 25 unique records and exact W/U/B/R/G decisions; tier/status consistency, closest-alternative/rationale presence, and all decision source/card references passed. `no_support_in_sample` records rely on completed scoped queries (PowerShell initially treated boolean `true` as non-string; direct inspection resolved that QA-script diagnostic). Capture/scoping review preserved released/digital/preview/playtest separation, face attribution, Colorless separation, user-selected Annihilator, limited-sample Absorb and unresolved Affinitycycling/Aura Swap. No additional unsupported first-release claim was found in the five Secondary records reviewed.

Bundled Python `tools/check_recon.py` passed source, taxonomy and fixture gates plus all 53 assertions. Both changed harnesses passed bundled Node syntax. # Prior handoff - Repeatable supplemental assessment protocol

## Control
- Status: `qa-passed` (qa-failed -> dev-in-progress -> ready-for-qa -> qa-passed after clarifying the one null-semantics sentence). Only the coordinator or Owner may mark complete.
- Branch: `codex/a-mechanics-review`; baseline `82c8add`; total branch baseline `9a063b3a98cfcb7672783f83a102e8f968242490`.
- Exact allowed files: `HANDOFF.md`, `AGENTS.md`, `docs/mechanics-research/ASSESSMENT-PROTOCOL.md`, `docs/mechanics-research/ASSESSMENT-TEMPLATE.json`.
- Scope: write a versioned supplemental research decision protocol, reusable research-record template, and a narrow discoverability/usage pointer in AGENTS. These are research workflow documents; no runtime schema migration, data reassessment, application change, 2021 edit, deployment or publication.

## Acceptance criteria
1. Fixed evidence hierarchy and explicit rule for direct official tiers versus contextual inference, card-derived judgment and user-selected classification; wiki is a locator, Scryfall verifies card facts, counts alone do not assign tiers.
2. Fixed scope, queries, deduplication, card-role/color attribution, chronology, rarity and special-material handling, complete saved-source receipts/PDF practice, and repeatable evidence table.
3. Ordered tier decision rules with a bounded small-sample path, confidence per color, unassigned versus not-applicable/conflicted distinctions, closest-alternative reasoning and no arbitrary scoring.
4. Mandatory player-facing rationale structure, source/decision provenance, versioned revisions and independent review gate; no silently changed criteria between letters or retroactive certification of existing A work.
5. Machine-readable blank template matches the documented protocol and worked cases explain Afflict, Absorb, Annihilator, Affinitycycling and generic rules actions without silently changing existing placements.
- QA: QA-0 documentation/template syntax plus independent consistency review because the document governs future judgment. Check JSON shape and links, compare worked cases with saved records, and Git scope/whitespace. No app/browser/gate sweep is necessary when runtime/data remain unchanged.

## Implementation / QA
- QA repair: replaced ambiguous `None means` with explicit `None of these statuses means the color can never receive the mechanic.` No decision rule, template or other document content changed. Rechecked whitespace and protected implementation diff; both clean.
- Added protocol v1.0, a blank JSON research-record template, and the scoped AGENTS usage pointer. The protocol fixes evidence routes, scope/capture/screening steps, qualitative tier tests, per-color alternatives/confidence/status, small-sample limits, rationale format and revision/review rules. The coordinator authored the protocol and final consistency corrections; the separate RobDev template worker authored only the template and reported JSON/field/whitespace checks.
- Exact implementation envelope remains four files. No application, assessment source/payload, generator, 2021 data or existing evidence changed. Earlier A work requires a bounded conformance review before claiming v1.0 compliance; this task performs no reassessment. In particular, a singleton Colorless example proves access, not a tier without the specified additional support or user decision.

- Focused real-browser evidence: bundled Node `tools/check_assessment_browser.mjs --playwright <bundled playwright> --channel msedge` exited 0 against the source checkout and again against the exact stamped `_site` package. It observed 25 assessments/70 tiers, two Colorless classifications, three rules actions and the scoped 2017 Attach note; frozen self-checks 84/84, Article 216, A-Z 543 + Special 44, unchanged math/non-A behavior, keyboard close and 360px containment; atomic missing/bad-tier/wrong-binding/unsafe-URL rejection; delayed refresh/input focus; and escaped source HTML. No screenshot regression or broad viewport sweep was run.

### Findings

1. **major — source binding is not checkout-independent, violating deterministic reproduction/source binding acceptance.** Reproduction: with `core.autocrlf=true`, hash each retained research source as working-copy bytes, then hash `git show 551f3331:<path>` bytes. Expected: a source binding derived from the committed semantic JSON is stable in a fresh LF checkout. Actual: all three differ solely by line endings: main assessment working `522bd8f6...1558` vs Git blob `c33cfeaa...5555`; Absorb `7db5bfc7...d46c` vs `02bcdccb...d5a4`; addendum `b4929faf...e1e` vs `4e0c0e45...1c69`. Working files contain 73,188/165/176 CRLF sequences while Git blobs contain zero. Canonical parsed-JSON hashes match across both representations (`ba029427...0013`, `3c21ac0d...5414`, `e530c771...79d`), proving content equality and the narrow repair path. Impact: a fresh Linux/LF checkout regenerates different `sourceBindings` and fails `--check`, so the candidate is not deterministically reproducible from the committed sources.
2. **major — Annihilator loses the required owner-directed classification authority in the generated payload and UI.** Reproduction: open Annihilator in the exact local preview. Expected from acceptance criterion 3 and the retained addendum: identify Primary Colorless / Secondary None / Tertiary None as the Owner/user-directed classification, while describing the supporting rationale as Scryfall-derived; keep Affinitycycling separately provisional/playtest-only. Actual: the panel's accessible label and visible label are `Scryfall-derived assessment - provisional`, it says `Analyst assessment`, and it contains no Owner/user-directed wording. The only owner-directed text remains in the unshipped source addendum, while the producer overwrites that label. Impact: the player-facing page misstates the decision authority and fails the explicit owner-directed/provisional/playtest distinction even though the tiers themselves are correct.

### Rework boundary and Owner checklist

- RobDev repair is limited to (1) canonical parsed-JSON source binding hashes plus regenerated payload/check expectations, and (2) preserving/rendering the Annihilator owner-directed classification label without weakening the separate Affinitycycling provisional/playtest label. Rework QA should rerun only deterministic generation/hash comparison and the focused Annihilator/Affinitycycling browser criterion, plus the required recon check if the candidate changes.
- Owner review is deferred until QA passes. After repair: (1) open Annihilator and confirm the classification is visibly owner-directed while its evidence rationale remains bounded; (2) open Affinitycycling and confirm it remains provisional, low-confidence and playtest-only. No appearance acceptance is inferred.

## Acceptance criteria

Unresolved evidence remains explicit. No inferred card legality or mechanics.

Official digital, card-specific, Un/special-format and narrow discussion scopes are preserved where supported. 2021 records keep original presentation; mapped supplemental evidence is classified separately.
3. All587 destinations remain discoverable without duplicates or lost exact matches under color lenses. Article remains216. A–Z may be grouped by evidence/scope with alphabetical order inside each group, replacing the prior single undifferentiated alphabet. No supplemental rankings feed math.
4. Runtime classification is local, versioned, fully accounted and bound to unchanged transfer inputs. Missing/malformed/mismatched scope data discloses supplemental-unavailable and keeps2021 usable, never presents unclassified supplemental entries as established.
5. XLSX and Markdown cover all entries and expose eligibility/scope prominently. Existing Owner input is preserved unless it is the explicitly generated13 suggestions; narrow-scope suggestions must not imply approved ordinary examples. Those remain clearly marked review-only or separated from ordinary selection work. Keep distinct identities. Files stay local and do not sync automatically.
6. Required gates, transfer integrity, deterministic audit checks and proportional packaged browser checks pass. Independent QA4 evaluates classifications against evidence, not only producer self-consistency. No publication; exact local candidate plus short Owner checklist.

## Plan / roles
Read-only source inventory and separate evidence critique first; coordinator fixes classification policy and owns producer/data/report/review files. RobDev owns HTML/harness/package after policy is grounded. Independent RobQA after committed ready-for-qa writes HANDOFF only. Available Codex fallback for unavailable prescribed Claude agents.
Baseline/post gate chain and transfer-check; record-by-record audit and independent exception checks; verify fullbrowse/search coverage, first-visible scope labels, mapped2021/conflicts, font/focus/lens/failure safety; package explicitallowlist; workbook inspect/render/recalculate/preservation and MD parity.

## Decisions and limitations
No new research is authorized or needed to distinguish the retained evidence. The audit classifies what the pinned accepted records support, not exhaustive MTG coverage or current tournament legality. Ambiguity is an explicit result, not automatic establishment. Owner's request authorizes clear separation and review-file corrections.
